The sustained pressure to grow her business, maintain her reputation for meticulous design work, and stay visible in the local arts scene gradually eroded her sleep schedule and left little time for rest or family life. Over the following months, coworkers and her husband noticed she became increasingly withdrawn on job sites, missed design details she normally prized, and started canceling theater commitments, yet she pushed herself harder to keep contracts from slipping. The condition became clinically significant when she began losing clients due to repeated mistakes and no‑shows, and after a tense meeting where a long‑time builder questioned her reliability, she broke down in front of her pastor, who urged her to see a psychiatrist when she described her internal experiences and how she could no longer trust her own judgment at work.\n- Chief Complaint: “I feel like there’s something going on behind the scenes on my jobs, like people are plotting around my designs, and no one can convince me otherwise.”\n▸ Patient-specific Symptom Manifestations\n- S019 (Delusions): On almost every job now I’m convinced the builders and even the homeowners are secretly emailing each other about how to sabotage my layouts or steal my design ideas, and even when my husband shows me normal messages or contracts I still feel like there’s a separate chain I’m not allowed to see where they’re laughing at me and planning to push me out of the business.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): When I’m alone in a house laying tile or trying to sketch a design, I hear this man’s voice right behind me saying things like, “You’re messing it up again,” or “They all know you’re a fraud,” even though there’s nobody there and the job site is completely empty.\n- S021 (Disorganized_Thinking_Speech): My husband and clients tell me I jump all over the place when I’m talking now, like I’ll start explaining a flooring estimate, then suddenly go off about how the theater director stole my scene ideas, then something about my pastor’s sermon, and I can see from their faces they’re lost but I can’t seem to pull my thoughts back to the original point.\n- S022 (Disorganized_Abnormal_Behavior): There are days on site where I just freeze in the middle of a room, holding a tile or a trowel, staring at the floor for long stretches and not responding when people call my name, and other times I’ll pace back and forth in the hallway, muttering to myself and checking the windows over and over like someone’s watching us from outside.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Most of the time now I feel like a shell going through the motions—my face feels stuck, I barely talk unless someone drags words out of me, and even simple things like showering, answering texts from friends, or hugging my kids feel like they take more effort than I have.\n- S025 (Loss_of_Interest): I used to lose hours happily painting or rehearsing lines at the community theater, but over the last year I’ve stopped showing up, my canvases are gathering dust in the studio, and even landing a big contract doesn’t excite me anymore—I just feel flat and detached, like none of it matters.\n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): I mostly just pick at food now and have to force down a few bites at dinner because I have no appetite, and my clothes hang looser on me even though I’m not trying to lose weight.\n- S029 (Fatigue): No matter how much I sleep or lie on the couch, I wake up feeling like I ran a marathon the day before, and climbing a flight of stairs or unloading a few boxes of tile leaves me so drained I have to sit down and rest in the middle of the workday.\n- S030 (Worthlessness_Guilt): I walk into these expensive homes and feel like a total failure who has ruined every project I’ve touched, and I can’t shake this belief that I somehow caused all the problems in my family and even the financial stress at church, like my bad energy or decisions have poisoned everything around me and I deserve to be punished for it.\n- S032 (Death_Suicide): Pretty much every day I catch myself thinking it would be better for everyone if I just didn’t wake up, and on my darkest days I’ve pictured driving my truck off the highway on the way to a job and felt almost relieved at the idea, even though actually doing it scares me enough that I end up calling my pastor or my husband instead.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_depressive_episode_occur_concurrently: When the voice is telling me I’m a fraud and I’m convinced the contractors are plotting against me, I’m also at my lowest—barely eating, dragging myself out of bed, and feeling completely hopeless about my work and my life at the same time.\n- depressive_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness_(Criterion_C): Looking back over this whole stretch, the heavy depression—the emptiness, guilt, and not caring about painting or theater—has been there almost the entire time, even during the few short spells when the paranoia and voices quieted down a bit.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode_(Criterion_B): There was a period late last summer when my energy and appetite had picked up a little and I felt less weighed down, but I was still absolutely convinced people were emailing behind my back about my designs and I kept hearing that man’s voice criticizing me on job sites.\n- functional_impairment: In the past year I’ve lost several big clients from missed appointments and half-finished jobs, pulled away from friends and theater completely, and my husband has had to take over a lot of the bills and household tasks because I can’t manage everything reliably anymore.\n- duration: This all really started to snowball not long after I took on those high-end contracts and began cramming for my licensing exam, so it’s been going on for a little over a year now.\n- psychotic_symptoms_duration: The paranoia about people plotting around my designs and the man’s voice criticizing me have been a regular part of my life for most of this past year, starting a few months after the work stress peaked.\n- depressive_episode_duration: My mood has been low, with that empty, guilty feeling and loss of interest in art and theater, for roughly the past year or so, ever since the long stretch of overworking and late nights began catching up with me.\n\nRules:\n- If He says awful things like, \"You're messing it up again,\" or \"They all know you're a fraud,\" and it's really hard to admit because I know there's nobody actually there.\n\nDoctor: Have you also experienced significant periods of feeling very depressed, down, or unusually energetic and high alongside these experiences?\n\nPatient: I’ve felt extremely depressed, empty, and hopeless for most of this past year, especially when the voices and paranoia are at their worst. I definitely don't feel high or energetic—it's the exact opposite, like I'm completely drained and flat. This heavy depression has been there almost the entire time.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever had a period of two weeks or more where you heard the voices or felt people were plotting against you, but your mood felt completely normal and you weren't feeling depressed?\n\nPatient: Yes, late last summer there was a period where my mood and energy lifted a bit and I felt less weighed down. But even then, I was still hearing that man's voice criticizing me and I was completely convinced the contractors were plotting and emailing behind my back about my designs."

(Delusions): On almost every job now I’m convinced the builders and even the homeowners are secretly emailing each other about how to sabotage my layouts or steal my design ideas, and even when my husband shows me normal messages or contracts I still feel like there’s a separate chain I’m not allowed to see where they’re laughing at me and planning to push me out of the business.\n- S020 (Hallucinations): When I’m alone in a house laying tile or trying to sketch a design, I hear this man’s voice right behind me saying things like, “You’re messing it up again,” or “They all know you’re a fraud,” even though there’s nobody there and the job site is completely empty.\n- S021 (Disorganized_Thinking_Speech): My husband and clients tell me I jump all over the place when I’m talking now, like I’ll start explaining a flooring estimate, then suddenly go off about how the theater director stole my scene ideas, then something about my pastor’s sermon, and I can see from their faces they’re lost but I can’t seem to pull my thoughts back to the original point.\n- S022 (Disorganized_Abnormal_Behavior): There are days on site where I just freeze in the middle of a room, holding a tile or a trowel, staring at the floor for long stretches and not responding when people call my name, and other times I’ll pace back and forth in the hallway, muttering to myself and checking the windows over and over like someone’s watching us from outside.\n- S023 (Negative_Symptoms): Most of the time now I feel like a shell going through the motions—my face feels stuck, I barely talk unless someone drags words out of me, and even simple things like showering, answering texts from friends, or hugging my kids feel like they take more effort than I have.\n- S025 (Loss_of_Interest): I used to lose hours happily painting or rehearsing lines at the community theater, but over the last year I’ve stopped showing up, my canvases are gathering dust in the studio, and even landing a big contract doesn’t excite me anymore—I just feel flat and detached, like none of it matters.\n- S026 (Weight_Appetite_Change): I mostly just pick at food now and have to force down a few bites at dinner because I have no appetite, and my clothes hang looser on me even though I’m not trying to lose weight.\n- S029 (Fatigue): No matter how much I sleep or lie on the couch, I wake up feeling like I ran a marathon the day before, and climbing a flight of stairs or unloading a few boxes of tile leaves me so drained I have to sit down and rest in the middle of the workday.\n- S030 (Worthlessness_Guilt): I walk into these expensive homes and feel like a total failure who has ruined every project I’ve touched, and I can’t shake this belief that I somehow caused all the problems in my family and even the financial stress at church, like my bad energy or decisions have poisoned everything around me and I deserve to be punished for it.\n- S032 (Death_Suicide): Pretty much every day I catch myself thinking it would be better for everyone if I just didn’t wake up, and on my darkest days I’ve pictured driving my truck off the highway on the way to a job and felt almost relieved at the idea, even though actually doing it scares me enough that I end up calling my pastor or my husband instead.\n▸ Additional Diagnostic Requirements\n- psychotic_symptoms_and_depressive_episode_occur_concurrently: When the voice is telling me I’m a fraud and I’m convinced the contractors are plotting against me, I’m also at my lowest—barely eating, dragging myself out of bed, and feeling completely hopeless about my work and my life at the same time.\n- depressive_episode_is_present_for_the_majority_of_the_total_duration_of_the_active_and_residual_portions_of_the_illness_(Criterion_C): Looking back over this whole stretch, the heavy depression—the emptiness, guilt, and not caring about painting or theater—has been there almost the entire time, even during the few short spells when the paranoia and voices quieted down a bit.\n- delusions_and_hallucinations_occur_for_2_or_more_weeks_in_the_absence_of_a_major_mood_episode_(Criterion_B): There was a period late last summer when my energy and appetite had picked up a little and I felt less weighed down, but I was still absolutely convinced people were emailing behind my back about my designs and I kept hearing that man’s voice criticizing me on job sites.\n- Subtypes: Cleaning or Washing Compulsions, Checking Compulsions, Ordering and Arranging Compulsions, Mental Acts, Repeating Rituals\n[S067] Preoccupation_with_Appearance: Preoccupation with one or more perceived defects or flaws in physical appearance that are not observable or appear slight to others. Subtypes: Intrusive Memories, Distressing Dreams, Dissociative Reactions (Flashbacks), Psychological Distress at Cues, Physiological Reactions to Cues\n[S070] Trauma_Avoidance: Persistent avoidance of stimuli associated with the traumatic event.  Subtypes: Internal Avoidance, External Avoidance\n[S071] Trauma_Related_Amnesia: Inability to remember an important aspect of the traumatic event (typically due to dissociative amnesia and not to other factors like head injury or alcohol).  Subtypes: Fragmented Memory, Generalized Amnesia\n[S072] Negative_Beliefs: Persistent and exaggerated negative beliefs or expectations about oneself, others, or the world. Subtypes: Self-Blame, Blaming Others\n[S074] Persistent_Negative_Emotional_State: Persistent negative emotional state (e.g., fear, horror, anger, guilt, or shame).  Subtypes: Persistent Fear, Persistent Horror, Persistent Anger, Persistent Guilt, Persistent Shame\n[S075] Social_Detachment: Feelings of detachment or estrangement from others.  Subtypes: Emotional Detachment, Social Isolation, Difficulty with Intimacy, Loss of Belonging\n[S076] Inability_Positive_Emotions: Persistent inability to experience positive emotions (e.g., inability to experience happiness, satisfaction, or loving feelings).  Subtypes: Anhedonia for Happiness, Lack of Satisfaction, Inability to Feel Love, Emotional Numbness\n[S077] Irritability_Aggression: Irritable behavior and angry outbursts (with little or no provocation) typically expressed as verbal or physical aggression.  Subtypes: Impulsive Anger, Verbal Aggression, Physical Aggression, Chronic Irritability\n[S078] Reckless_Self_Destructive_Behavior: Reckless or self-destructive behavior pattern that endangers one's safety or health.
